eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟātu annāṭṭuka karuṅkaḷamar mutalāyiṉār varuntāmaṟ peṟum poruḷ piṟanāṭṭārkku viruntuceyyat tarumeṉṟu annāṭṭiṉatu vaḷami kūṟiṉamiyiṉeṉpatu.3 tōḻi poruntiyavaḻik kūṟumāṟu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cevili poruntu vaḻik kūṟuvaṉavuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka, ilakkaṇa muṇmiyiṉ aviyum uḷaveṉpatu karuttu.	(31)
āyvuri
itu, tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅ kūṟutaṟkuriya iṭam uṇarttukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ) tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum kālattiṟkum, iṭattiṟkum kūṟakkarutiya poruṭkum poruntu māṟu nōkkik kēṭpōr uyttuṇarntu koḷḷutaṟkuriya neṟiyāl uḷḷuṟi yuvamam kūṟutaṟkuriyar e-ṟu.
poruntuvaḻinōkkik koḷvaḻiyāṉa (uḷḷuṟiyuvamam) kūṟutaṟkuriyar eṉa iyiyum, uḷḷuṟiyuvamam eṉpatu atikārattāl vantiyintatu. ivvāṟu akattiṇiyoḻukalāṟṟil uḷḷuṟiyuvamam kūṟutaṟkuriyār iṉṉiṉṉār eṉavitantu eṭuttōtavē iṅkuk kūṟappaṭāta talimakaḷ tāyākiya naṟṟāyum āyattārum tantiyum tamiyaṉmārum uḷḷuṟiyuvami kūṟappeṟār eṉpatum, iṅṅaṉam uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅkūṟutal akattiṇi yoḻukalāṟṟiṟpōlap puṟattiṇiyoḻukalāṟṟil attuṇi iṉṟiyāmiyāta taṉṟātaliṉ, ivvuḷḷuṟi yuvamattiṉi akattiṇikkē ciṟappurimiyuṭiyatākat tolkāppiyaṉār eṭuttōtiṉāreṉpatum naṉku pulaṉām.
34.	vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamit tōṟṟam
kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻik koḷāal.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉ--eṉiṉ. mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām puṟamamiyuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īṇṭu eṭuttōtappaṭṭa ilakkaṇattiṉ vēṟu paṭṭuvanta uvamittōṟṟam eṭuttōtiya neṟiyiṟ koḷḷum vaḻik koḷuvuka eṉṟavāṟu.
		parutiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉik
kiruḷvaḷi vuṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻil
eṉavarum.1 piṟavumaṉṉa.
pērāciriyam
itu, mēlellām iruvakiyuvamam kūṟi iṉṉum ēṉiyuvamap pakutiyē kūṟuvāṉ eytātateytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ) vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟam- vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟa vanta uvamiccāti; tōṟṟameṉiṉum piṟappeṉiṉuñ cāti yeṉiṉum okkum;2 kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻi koḷāal-aṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭa vantaṉavāyiṉum mēṟkūṟiya pakutiyāvē koḷḷumiṭaṉaṟintu koḷuttuka.3 (v-W).
koḷuvu taleṉpatu koḷā al eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
vēṟupaṭavarutaleṉpatu *uvamikkum poruṭkum oppumi māṟupaṭak kūṟutalum, oppumikūṟātu peyarpōl vaṉavaṟṟu māttiriyāṉē maṟuttuk kūṟutalum, oppumi maṟuttup poruḷi nāṭṭik kūṟutalum, oppumi maṟutta vaḻip piṟitōruvami nāṭṭutalum. uvamiyum poruḷum muṟkūṟi niṟī ip piṉṉar maṟṟiya ovvāveṉṟalum, uvamikku irukuṇaṅ koṭuttup poruḷiṉi vāḷātu kūṟuṅkāl uvamiyiṉi iraṇṭākki oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiya aṭi oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟātalum, oppumi kuṟivupaṭa uvamittu maṟṟoru kuṇaṅkoṭuttu nirapputalum, **ovvākkaruttiṉāṉ oppumi kōṭalum, uvamattiṟkaṉṟi uvamattiṟku ētuvākiya poruṭkuc cila aṭikūṟi av aṭiyāṉē uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉic ciṟappittalum, uvamāṉattiṉi uvamēyamākkiyum atu vilakkiyuṅ kūṟutalum, iraṇṭu poruḷāṉē vevvēṟu kūṟiyavaḻi oṉṟu oṉṟaṟku uvamiyeṉpatu koḷḷa vittalum, iṉṉōraṉṉa vellām vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟam eṉappaṭum.
ivaṟṟik kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷuttutal eṉpatu muṟkūṟiya ēṉiyuvamattiṉpālum piṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟiyuvamattiṉpālum paṭuttu uṇarappaṭumeṉpatu. ēṉiyuvamattiṉ pāṟpaṭutta leṉpatu:viṇi payaṉ mey uru eṉṟa  nāṉkumpaṟṟi (276) varutalum avaṟṟukku ōtiya aivaki
nilikkaḷaṉumpaṟṟi (279-280) varutalumeṉak koḷka. uḷḷuṟiyuvamattiṉ pāṟpaṭuttaleṉpatu; ivvēṉiyuvamam pōla uvamiyum poruḷumāki vēṟuvēṟu viḷaṅka vārātu kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷavarutaliṉ ikkaruttiṉāṉē itaṉi īṇṭu vippāṉāyiṟṟu varalāṟu:
		viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu
iṭañciṟi teṉṉum ūkkan turappa
voṭuṅkā vuḷḷat tōmpā vīkik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēra lātaṉi
yāṅṅaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam
poḻuteṉa variti puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti
māṟi varuti malimaṟin toḷitti
akaliru vicumpi ṉāṉum
*pakalviḷaṅ kaliyāṟ palkatir virittē	(puṟam.8)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ  kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēralātaṉi eṉṉun tuṇi uvamattiṟku vanta aṭiyiṉip poruṭku maṟuttuk koḷḷa vittāṉeṉpatu.1 eṉṉi? veñcuṭar vaḻi eṉṉun tuṇi uvamattiṟkuriya aṭiyiṉip poruṭku maṟuttuk koḷḷa vittāṉeṉpatu.2 iṉip poḻuteṉa variti eṉpatu toṭaṅkip pāṭṭu muṭikāṟum poruṭkuriya aṭiyiṉi uvamattiṟku maṟuttuk koḷḷavittāṉeṉpatu, eṉṉi? veñcuṭar vaḻit tōṉṟiya aracaṉit taṇcuṭarōṭu paḻippāṉ 3 poruḷē uvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉu(234) meṉṟataṉāṟ poruḷiṉi uvamiyākak kūṟātu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam pōlak koḷḷa vittup piṉṉar uvamattiṟku aṭiyāyavaṟṟuḷ,
viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻuka.
eṉṟāṉ.
vaḻi moḻitaleṉpatu: vēṟṟaracarkkut tam taṉmiyeṉa vēṟiṉṟit taṉṉakappaṭuttal; ākalāṉ, tattam oḷiyoṭu paṭuttatu,oḻinta kōḷuñ cellat tāṉuñcellum matiyameṉṟu etirmaṟuttuk kuṟṟaṅkūṟic kuṟippuppaṭa vittāṉeṉpatu.
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu
eṉavē, iṉpanukarvu muṟṟucciṟappillāk kaṭṭuri yeytāṉeṉavum avaṉōṭu uvamikkiṉṟa matiyamāyiṉ irupatteḻuvar makaḷi roṭu pōkaṅtuyttuc ciṟappillāta kaṭṭuri puṉiyumeṉṟum etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷavittāṉ. ciṟappiṉmiyeṉpatu, ellārkkum ottavāṟṟāṉ aṟañceyyātu urōkiṇimēṟ kaḻiperuṅ kātalaṉeṉappaṭutal pōlvaṉa.
 iṭañciṟi teṉṉu mūkkan turappa
eṉavē, eññāṉṟun taṉṉellikkaṇṇē varum matimaṇṭala meṉṟu etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭum, oṭuṅkāvuḷḷam eṉavē, ammatiyam tēyntoṭuṅkumeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. ōmmā vīki eṉavē, nāṭōṟum orōvoru kaliyākap palluyirkkum iṉpam payakkumāṟṟāl taruvatallātu tāṉuṭiyavellām orukālē koṭāta matiyameṉappaṭum. kaṭantaṭu tāṉi eṉavē, matikkut tāṉiyākiya tarākiyellām pakikkatirākiya paritimaṇṭilattukkut tōṟkumeṉṟāṉām. ivvāṟṟāṉ uvamāṉa aṭiyellām etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉip, poruṭkuriya aṭiyum avvāṟu etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭu meṉṟavāṟu, vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭila meṉavē, kaṭiyāyiṉār katiyiṟ cellu matiyameṉṟu pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ taliyāyiṉār katiyiṟ cellumeṉṟāṉ, poḻuteṉa variti yeṉavē, nāṭōṟum nāḻiki vēṟupaṭṭu eṟitti eṉṟataṉāṉ ivaṉ poḻutu ceyyāṉeṉavum, puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti yeṉavē, tōṟṟōr pōṉṟu oḷi maḻuṅkik ceṟikiṉṟāyeṉavum, viḷaṅkittōṉṟa veṉpataṉāl toṭarkiṉṟa cuṭar pōla viḷaṅkip piṟar tōṟṟōṭakkāyum ivaṉeṉavum, māṟi varuti yeṉavē, tiṅkaṭōṟummāṟip piṟatti yeṉpataṉāṉ ivaṉ nilipeṟṟāṉeṉavum, mali maṟin toḷitti yeṉavē malicārntavaḻit tōṉṟāyeṉ pataṉāṉ ivaṉ taṉṉāṭṭu malimīk kūṟumeṉavum, akaliru vicumpiṉāṉu meṉavē, ivaṉ ivvulakattu nilipēṟuṭiya ṉeṉavum, pakalviḷaṅkāyeṉavē, ivaṉ irupoḻutum viḷaṅkumeṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. muṟpakutiyum piṟpakutiyum vēṟupaṭutaliṉ vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu: maṟṟiyavum aṉṉa.
ikkūṟiya aṭi yellām, viṉi payaṉ meyuruveṉak kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻik koḷuvuvatākak kāṭṭuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum.
		kaṇṇa ṉavaṉivaṉ māṟaṉ kamaḻ tuḻāyk
kaṇṇi yavaṟkivaṟku vēmputār--vaṇṇamum
māya ṉavaṉivaṉ cēyaṉ marapoṉṟē
āya viṉivaṉ kō
eṉpatu,peyantāru mutalāyiṉapaṟṟi māyaṉōṭu uvamaṅkaruti maṟutturittavāṟu.1 peyareṉpatu poruḷuṇarttuliṉ ataṉi vaṭiviṉpāṟ paṭuttuṇarka.
		aṭinōkki ṉāḻkaṭal vaṇṇaṉṟaṉ mēṉip
paṭinōkkiṟ piṅkoṉṟit tārōṉ-- muṭinōkkit 
tērvaḷava ṉāta ṟeḷintēṉ ṟaṉ ceṉṉimēl
āravaṅka ṟōṉ ṟiṟṟuk kaṇṭu	(taṇṭiyalaṅ-pā.39)
eṉpataṉuḷ, āḻkaṭal vaṇṇaṉiyuṅ koṉṟittārōṉiyum uvami kūṟiyavaṟṟi2 maṟuttut tērvaḷavaṉeṉat teḷintēṉeṉap poruḷiyē nāṭutaliṉ aḵtu uvamam vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu.
		intira ṉeṉṉi ṉiraṇṭēkaṇ ṇēṟūrnta
vantarattā ṉeṉṉiṟ piṟiyilli--yantarattuk
kōḻiyā ṉeṉṉiṉ mukamoṉṟē kōtiyi
āḻiyā ṉeṉṟuṇaraṟ pāṟṟu	(muttoḷḷāyiram)  
eṉpataṉuḷ intiraṉiyum, iṟiyōṉiyum, murukaṉiyum,oppu maṟuttu neṭiyōṉi uvamaṅkūṟaliṉ oppumi maṟuttup piṟitu nāṭṭiyatu.
		cuṟṟuviṟ kāmaṉuñ cōḻar perumakaṉāṅ
koṟṟappōrka kiḷḷiyuṅ kēḻovvār--poṟṟoṭīi
yāḻi yuṭiyāṉ makaṉmāyaṉ cēyaṉē
kōḻi yuṭiyāṉ makaṉ	(taṇṭi-pā-52)
eṉpataṉāl, uvamiyuṅ poruḷum muṉ oruṅkukiṟī ippiṉṉar ovvāmi kūṟutaliṉ ituvum piṉṉum vēṟupaṭavantatāyiṟṟu.
		puṉaṉāṭar kōmāṉum pūntuḻāy mālum
viṉivakiyāṉ vēṟu paṭuva--puṉaṉāṭa
ṉēṟṟeṟintu māṟṟalarpā leytiyapār māyavaṉ
ēṟṟirantu koṇṭamiyā ṉiṉṟu	(taṇṭi-pā-53)
eṉpatum  atu.
		ūrkkuṟu mākkaḷ veṇkōṭu kaḻāaliṉ
nīrttuṟi paṭiyum peruṅkaḷiṟu pōla
iṉiyi peruma emakkē	(puṟam.94)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ uvamiyākiya poruḷiṉi yāṉiyuṅ kaṭāmumeṉa iraṇṭākki yāṉikkē ūrkkuṟumākkaḷ veṇkōṭukaḻāliṉ nīrttuṟipaṭiyumeṉṉum aṭikūṟi ūrkkuṟumākkaḷ pōlvārit tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭāattiṟkuc collāmiyiṉ ituvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamattōṟṟa meṉappaṭṭatu.
		mutirkōṅkiṉmukiyeṉa mukañceyta kurumpayeṉap
peyaṟuḷi mukiḻeṉap peruttaniṉ ṉiḷa muli	(kali.56)
eṉṟavaḻi, mutirkōṅkiṉ mukiyum muṟṟiya kurumpiyiyum periyavākaliṉ avipōlap perutta niṉ iḷamuliyeṉṟal ottatu, peyaṟuḷimukuḷañ ciṟitākavum ivaṟṟōṭu ataṉi uṭaṉkūṟi appeyaṟuḷi mukuḷattiṟkillāta perumikkuṇam poruṭkup piṉṉar vitantu kūṟutaliṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamiyāyiṟṟu.
		makkaḷē pōlvar kayava ravaraṉṉa
voppāri yāṅkaṇṭatil	(kuṟaḷ.1071)
eṉpatu ovvāpporuḷi oppumikoṇṭatu. eṉṉi?  makkaḷik kayavar ovvāreṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṉ makkaḷ pōlvar kaṇavareṉṟamiyiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamiyāṟṟu.
		neṭuntōṭṭirumpaṉi nīrniḻal puriyak
kuṟumpala murinta kuṉṟucērciṟuneṟi
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamiyākiya niḻal poruṭkeytiyatu uvama viṉiyaṉṟē, ataṟku neṭuntōṭum perumiyum aṭiyākak kūṟiṉāṉ; kūṟavē, paṉiniḻalōṭokkuṅ kuṉṟañcēr ciṟuneṟi muṭintavaḻic ceṉṟupukum ūrkkuvami neṭuntōṭaṭeṉṟu koḷḷa vittamiyiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamiyāyiṟṟu.
		maṇpaṭutōṭ kiḷḷi matavēḻa māṟṟaracar
veṇkuṭiyit tēytta vekuḷiyāl- viṇpaṭarntu
pāyuṅko leṉṟu paṉimatiyun taṉṉuruvan
tēyun teḷivicumpi ṉiṉṟu	(taṇṭi-pā.58)
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamāṉattiṉi uvamēyākki ataṉiyē vilakkiṉār. eṉṉi? veṇkuṭiyeṉṟu yāṉi kuttumeṉṟu matiyiṉik kuṭiyuṭaṉoppippāṉ matiyiṉik kuṭiyākavē  kūṟit tēyunteḷivicumpiṉiṉṟu eṉpataṉāṟ kuṭiyōṭu uvami kūṟiyati vilakkiṉamiyiṉ1 atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamat tōṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
		akara mutala veḻuttellām āki
pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē yulaku
eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭu poruḷ vēṟuvēṟu kūṟiyataṉṟi, akara mutal eḻuttellām atupōla eṉṟāṉum, ātipakavaṉ mutaṟṟu ulakam atupōla eṉṟāṉum oṉṟākat tuṇiyumāṟṟāṉ uvamiyum poruḷuṅ kūṟāmiyiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamamāyiṟṟu; piṟavum aṉṉa. ivi ēṉiyuvamattiṟkellām potuvilakkaṇam.
āyvuri
iḵtu, uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅkūṟuntiṟattiṉkaṇ talivaṉuk kuriyatōr marapu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) talimakaṉukkāyiṉ (avaṉkūṟum uḷḷuṟi iṉṉa poruṭkeṉṟu) iṭam variyaṟukkappaṭutal illi.e-ṟu.
eṉavē talivaṉ kūṟum uḷḷuṟi epporuṭkaṇṇum porunti varum eṉpatām.
itu, mēṟkuṟitta iruvakiyuvamaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya ēṉiyuvamaṅkaḷuṭ palvēṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷiyuṭiyaṉavākak kālantōṟuṅ kiḷittut tōṉṟum uvamip parappellām aṭaṅkakkoḷḷumāṟituveṉavuṇarttukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ) eṭuttōtap paṭṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil vēṟupāṭutōṉṟa vanta uvamappakutikaḷi avvāṟu vēṟupaṭa vantaṉavēṉum mēṟkūṟiya (ēṉiyuvamam uḷḷuṟiyuvamam eṉṉum) uvamap pakutiyuṭaṉ oppunōkki amittukkoḷḷum iṭamaṟintu poruntakkoḷka.
uvamikkum poruṭkum oppumi māṟupaṭak kūṟutalum, oppumi kūṟātu peyar mutaliyaṉa kūṟumaḷavil maṟuttuk kūṟutalum, oppumi maṟuttup poruḷi nāṭṭikkūṟutalum, oppumi maṟuttavaḻip piṟitōr uvamināṭṭutalum, uvamiyum poruḷum muṟkūṟiniṟuttip piṉṉar maṟṟiya ovvāveṉṟalum, uvamikku irukuṇaṅkoṭuttu vaṟitē kūṟumiṭattu uvamiyiṉi iraṇṭākki oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiya aṭimoḻi maṟṟoṉṟaṟkuk kūṟātu viṭutalum, oppumi kuṟivupaṭa uvamittu maṟṟoru kuṇaṅkoṭuttu nirapputalum, ovvākkaruttiṉāl oppumi koḷḷutalum,uvamattiṟkaṉṟi uvamattiṟkētuvākiya poruḷ kaḷukkuc cila aṭimoḻikūṟi avvaṭimoḻiyāṉē uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉic ciṟappittalum, uvamāṉattiṉi uvamēyamākkiyum atu vilakkiyum kūṟutalum, iraṇṭuporuḷālē vēṟu vēṟu kūṟiya vaḻi oṉṟu oṉṟaṟku uvamiyeṉpatu koḷḷavittalum ākiya ivi pōlvaṉavellām vēṟupaṭavanta uvamap pakutikaḷ eṉavum iviyellām viṉi payaṉ meyuru eṉṉum nāṉkum paṟṟi ēṉiyuvamattiṉ pālum, uvamiyum poruḷumāki vēṟu vēṟu viḷaṅkavārātu kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷa varutalpaṟṟi uḷḷuṟiyuvamattiṉpālum aṭaṅkap poruttavittu uṇarappaṭum eṉavum ivaṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭuttantu viḷakkuvar pērāciriyar. pērāciriyar kūṟum ivviḷakkamum eṭuttukkāṭṭuk kaḷum tolkāppiya uvamaviyaluṭaṉ piṟkālattuttōṉṟiya aṇiyiyali oppiṭṭu ārāytaṟkuriya neṟiyiṉip pulappaṭuttum muṟiyil amintuḷḷami uṇarntu makiḻattakuvatākum.
35.	orīik kūṟalum marīiya paṇpē.
iḷam pūraṇam
eṉ--eṉiṉ. ituvu muvamik kuriyatōr marapu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uvamiyi uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉ nīkkik kūṟalum maruviya iyalpu eṉṟavāṟu1.
kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēra lātaṉi (puṟam.8)
eṉa varum.
		aṉiccamum aṉṉattiṉ tūviyum mātar
aṭikku neruñcip paḻam	(kuṟaḷ. 1120)
eṉpatumatu.	(35)
pērāciriyam
ituvum, ēṉiyuvamattiṟkāvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) orīikkūṟalum1 ---okkumeṉak kūṟātu ovvā 
teṉak kūṟutalum, ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu; marīiya paṇpu-atuvum uvamiyātaṟku aṭippaṭavanta vaḻakku (e-ṟu.)
utāraṇam :
yāṅṅaṉa mottiyōvilaṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam	(puṟam.8)
eṉavum,
		mātar mukampō loḷiviṭa valliyēṟ
kātalivāḻi mati	(kuṟaḷ. 1118)
eṉavum varum.
niṉṉō raṉiyi nī
eṉpatum atu,
ivaṟṟuḷ, yāṅṅaṉam ottiyōveṉpatu ovvāyeṉṉum poruṭṭu. kātali vāḻimati; yeṉṟavaḻiyum yāṉ kātaliyāmi yāl matiyamē avaḷ vāṇmuka movvāyeṉṟaliṉ orī ik kūṟiṟṟu. ituvum marīiya paṇpākalāṉum uḷḷuṟi yuvamappōlak kuṟippiṉāṉ uvamaṅkōṭa loppumiyāṉum īṇṭu vittāṉeṉpatu. paṇpeṉṟataṉāṉ atuvum ilakkaṇat tōṭokkum eṉṟavāṟu. ivaṟṟiyum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamameṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, uvamiyum avvaḻi māṟupaṭa varumāṟu2 uvamattuk kūṟiṉāṉ, uvamiyiṉmi kūṟutalum uvami yeṉappaṭumeṉṟaṟku itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. ivaṟṟuḷḷum vēṟupaṭa vanta ilakkaṇam vēliccūttirattuḷ aṭaṅkum. 	    (33)
āyvuri
iḵtu, ēṉiyuvamattiṟkuriyatōr marapuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) uvamiyi uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉnīkkik kūṟutalum neṭuṅkālamāka nilipeṟṟu vaḻaṅkum uvamamarapākum e-ṟu
orīik kūṟal-nīkkik kūṟal.
oruvutal eṉṉumpakutinīṅkutal, nīkkutal eṉattaṉ viṉikkum piṟaviṉikkum potuvātaliṉ īṇṭu orīikkūṟal eṉṟatu nīkkik kūṟal eṉapporuḷ tantuniṉṟatu.
36.	uvamit taṉmiyum uritteṉa moḻipa
payaṉili purinta vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḷampūraṇam
eṉ--eṉiṉ. ituvu matu.
(i - ḷ.) uvamikkappaṭum poruḷōṭu uvamitōṉṟa varutalēyaṉṟi yuvamiyatu taṉmi kūṟalu muvamiyātaṟkurittu; payaṉili poruntiya vaḻakkiṉkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē ivvāṟu varuvatu payaṉili yuvamikkaṇ eṉṟu koḷka.1
		pāri pāri yeṉṟupala ētti
oruvaṟ pukaḻvar cennāp pulavar
pāri yoruvaṉum allaṉ
māriyu muṇṭīṇ ṭulakupurap patuvē.	(puṟam. 107)
itu pāri pōlum pāriyatu koṭieṉṉātu ivvāṟu kūṟum poruṇmiyum uvamamām eṉṟavāṟu.	(36)
pērāciriyam
iḵtu, eytātatu eytuvittatu; mēl ēṉiyuvamaṅ kūṟuṅ kāl uvamikkappaṭum poruṭkup perumiyuñ ciṟumiyuñ ciṟappakkūṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟāṉ, avvāṟaṉṟi uvamamuṅ koḷḷappaṭumeṉṟamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamattaṉmiyum uritteṉa moḻipa --- vikāra vakiyāṟ perumiyuñ ciṟumiyum oruporuṭkuk kūṟātu paṭṭāṅku uvamaṅkūṟutalum1 uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; payaṉili purinta vaḻakkattāṉa---ataṉāṉum oru payaṉ tōṉṟac colluta ṉeṟippāṭṭiṉkaṇ (e-ṟu.)
atu,
		pāri pāri yeṉṟu pala vētti
yoruvaṟ pukaḻvā cennāp pulavar	(puṟam. 107)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉaḷ ulakaḷittaṟku māriyum uṇṭeṉa māriyi uvamittuc ciṟappittukkūṟuvāṉ, māriyic ciṟappittup pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉākiyapāriyi uyarttukkūṟātāṉ pōla iyalpiṉāṉ uvami kūṟiṉāṉām;itu mārikkum pārikkumōriḻivilli yeṉṉun taṉmippaṭak kūṟavē avaṉuyarvu kūṟutaliṟ payaṉili purinta vaḻakkeṉap paṭumeṉpatu.2

		kolviṉip polinta kūrṅkuṟum puḻukiṉ
villōr tūṇi vīṅkap peyta
appunuṉi ēyppa arumpiya iruppic
ceppaṭa raṉṉa ceṅkuḻi yakantō

37.	taṭumāṟu varalum1 kaṭivari viṉṟē.

(i-ḷ.) uvamikkaṇ taṭumāṟu varutal nīkkappaṭātu eṉṟa vāṟu.2

pērāciriyam
ituvum, uvamattiṟkēyāvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) taṭumāṟuvamam---uvamiyum poruḷum vēṟu nīṟi i itupōlum ituveṉṉātu avviraṇṭiṉiyum uvamiyuṟac collun taṭumāṟuvamam; iṉi avvāṟaṉṟi uvamiyip poruḷākkiyum poruḷi uvami yākkiyun taṭumāṟac collutalun taṭumāṟuvamameṉappaṭum; kaṭivari iṉṟu---avviraṇṭum uvama meṉṟu collaṟ pāṭṭiṟ kaṭiyappaṭā (v-W).
		arimalar āyntaka ṇammā kaṭici
tirumukamun tiṅkaḷuñ cettut - terumantu

		taḷipeṟṟu vikiya taṇcuṉi nīla
maḷipeṟṟār kaṇpōṉ malaru---maḷipeṟṟa

kallāram pōṉmalaruṅ kaṇ
eṉpuḻi, nīlattōṭu kaṇṇiṉiyum kaṇṇiṉōṭu nīlattiṉiyum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu uvamiyākkiyum poruḷākkiyum oruṅkē taṭumāṟak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ ituvun taṭumāṟuvama meṉappaṭṭatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(35)
taṭumāṟu uvamam - uvami itu, uvamēyam itu eṉavēṟu niṟutti itu pōlvatu itu, eṉṟu kūṟātu avviraṇṭumē uvama mākat tōṉṟumpaṭi kūṟappaṭum uvamam; iṉi, uvamiyi uvamēyamākkiyum uvamēyatti uvamiyākkiyum taṭumāṟac collum uvamam taṭumāṟuvamam eṉiṉum ām.
kaṭivari iṉṟu - avviraṇṭum uvamam eṉṟu collun- tiṟattiṟ kaṭiyappaṭutal (nīkkappaṭutal) illi.
āyvuri

(i-ḷ) uvamiyum poruḷum vēṟuvēṟu niṟutti itupōlum ituveṉṟu kūṟātu avviraṇṭaṉuḷ uvamiyāvatu ētu uvamēyamāvatu etu eṉakkaṟpōr aiyuṟaccollutalum, uvamiyip poruḷākkip poruḷiyuvamiyākkic collu talum eṉa iṅṅaṉam taṭumāṟivarum uvamam uvamam eṉṟu collum nilimikkaṇ tavaṟeṉṟu vilakkappaṭātu. e-ṟu
iṅṅaṉam ceyyuḷik kaṟpōr aiyuṟṟut tērntu tuṇiyum kuṟippuṭaṉ uvamiyamitalum ceyyuḷiṉ poruḷāyvukku aḻaku ceyyumātalāl taṭumāṟuvamamtavaṟeṉṟu kaṭiyappaṭā teṉṟār.

āyvuri
itaṉ mutalaṭiyiṉi oru cūttiramākavum piṉṉiraṇṭaṭikaḷi maṟṟoru cūttiramākavum pirittu urivarintār pērāciriyar. avarkaruttuppaṭi ṅaa: aṭukkiya tōṟṟam viṭuttal paṇpē eṉattaṉic cūttiramākak koḷḷutalē tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēṟpuṭiyatākum.
iḵtu uvamikkuvami kūṟutal kuṟṟam eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aṭukakivaraluvamiyiṉivilakkutal uvamaṅkūṟuvōrk kuriya iyalpākum e-ṟu.
aṭukkivaraluvamiyāvatu, oruporuḷōṭuoru poruḷi yuva mavurupu tōṉṟa uvamittu. apporuḷōṭu piṟitoru poruḷi yuvamittu ivvāṟu uvamikaḷi uvamiyum poruḷumākat toṭarntaṭukki aviyaṉittiyum oru poruṭṭu uvamiyāka muṭittuk kūṟutal. iṅṅaṉam uvamiyum poruḷumāka aṭukkutal uvamiyum poruḷum eṉṟavakiyāl okkumāyiṉum tammuḷ vēṟupāṭuṭiya ivioru poruṭku uvamiyākavariṉ avaṟṟiṭiyēyaminta potuttaṉmiyākiya oppumik
kuṇam tammuḷmāṟupaṭṭup poruḷ mayakkamuṇṭāmātalāl aṭukkivaraluvamiyākiya atu koḷḷappaṭātu eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.

37.	niraṉiṟut tamittal niraṉiṟi cuṇṇam
vari nili vitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē.
ituvum eytātaṉa variyaṟiyuṭimiyiṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu iṉamākaliṉ uvamattiṟkāvatoṉ ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ.) niraṉiṟuttu amittal niraṉiṟi---uvamiyum niralē niṟuttup poruḷiṉiyum niralē niṟuttu. oppumi kūṟiṉ atu niraṉiṟiyeṉappaṭum; cuṇṇam varinili vitta mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē---cuṇṇattiṉi varinta nilimi yāṉ vanta cuṇṇamum aṭimaṟiyum moḻimāṟṟu meṉṉum mūṉṟumallāta iṭattu (e - ṟu.)
cuṇṇattiṉi varinta nilimiyāṉ vantaṉaveṉavē cuṇṇantavira mūṉṟuḷavōveṉiṉ ataṉi oruvari vilakka mūvarum vantilareṉṟāṟpōlak koḷka, utāraṇam :
		koṭikuvaḷi koṭṭinucuppuṇkaṇ mēṉi
matipavaḷa mutta mukamvāy muṟuval
piṭipiṇi maññi naṭinōkkuc cāyal
vaṭiviṉaḷē vañci makaḷ
eṉavarum.
niraṉiṟuttaleṉṉātu amitta leṉṟataṉāṉ niraṉiṟi yaṉṟi amittuk koḷvatum uṇṭu; atu,
		kaḷiṟuṅ kantum pōla naḷikaṭaṟ
kūmpuṅ kalaṉun tōṉṟun
tōṉṟaṉ maṟantōr tuṟikeḻu nāṭṭē
eṉa varum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
eṉṟārkku, niraṉiṟut tamittal niraṉiṟi ēṉi, vari nili vitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē eṉṉātu cuṇṇattiṉi varintōtiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi mūṉṟuñ cuṇṇam pōlac cuṇṇañ ceyyappaṭutaliṉ avvāṟṟāñ citarntu kiṭappa uvamiyuṅ kūṟiṉ atu poruḷ viḷakkāteṉpatu aṟivittaṟkeṉpatu. eṉṉi? aṭimaṟiyuḷ oraṭiyuḷ uvamaṅ kūṟi ōraṭiyuṭ poruḷvittāl iṉitu poruḷkoḷḷātu aṭimaṟikkuṅ kālip piṟitupiṟitāka luṭittu. iṉi ōraṭiyuḷ uvamaṅkūṟip piṉṉar ettuṇiyuñ ceṉṟu poruḷkūṟi moḻi māṟṟikkoḷḷa vippiṉ atuvum uvamattāṟ poruḷ tōṉṟātu. cuṇṇattiṟkum aḵtokkum.
iṉi, ivvōttiṉiṟ kūṟukiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cila vaṟṟiyuñ collatikārattiṉuḷḷuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷḷuñ collukiṉṟaṉa cila poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅkikkoṇṭu maṟṟavi ceyyuṭkaṇṇē aṇiyāmeṉa ikkālattāciriyar nūl brŒjhUKs®. avi oru taliyākac ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭā. eṉṉi? vallār ceyyiṉ aṇiyākiyum allār ceyyiṉ aṇiyaṉṟākiyum varun, tāṅkāṭṭiya ilakkaṇattiṟ citiyā vaḻiyumeṉpatu. eṉṉi?
		nāyakarkku nāykaḷpō ṉaṭpiṟ piṟaḻātu
kūuyk kuḻāa muṭaṉkoṭku---māypaṭi
paṉṟi yaṉiyar pakivēnta rāṅkavar
ceṉṟevaṉ ceyvar ceru
eṉṟavaḻi, nāypōlum naṭpuṭiyar paṭiyāḷareṉpatu viṉi yuvamam; paṉṟiyaṉiyar pakivēntareṉpatu nāykkup pakiyākiya paṉṟipōlaveṉpatu. vēṟṟuvēntar paki varātalāl avvuvami vilakkaritu aṉṟāmāyiṉum aḵtaṇi yeṉappāṭātu, uvamitāṉ uyarntatiṉmiyiṉ. atu kuṟṟa meṉṟōveṉiṉ,
		pērū raṭṭakaḷḷiṟku
ōril kōyiṟ ṟērumā ṉiṉṉē	(puṟam. 300)
eṉpatu, kuṟṟamaṉṟākaliṉ atuvuṅ kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉappaṭum. iṉip poṉmāliyum pūmāliyumpōlap polivu ceytaliṉ ituvuṅ kuṟṟamākātu mēlatē kuṟṟameṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟu iṉṉacollum iṉṉa poruḷumuṭiyaṉa poṉmāliyeṉavum pūmāliyeṉavum variyaṟuttuk kūṟalaṉmiyiṉ ataṉāṉum ataṉik kuṟṟameṉṟu ilakkaṇattāṟkūṟiṉ nirampātu. allatūum poruḷati kārattuṭ poruṭpakutikaḷellāñ ceyyuṭku aṇiyākalāṉ avi pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavi yeṉap paṭṭaṉa. eṉṟataṉāṉ, aviyellān tokuttu aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu vēṟu cilavaṟṟi varintu aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal payamil kūṟṟāmeṉpatu.
iṉi,iraṇṭu poruḷeṇṇiyavaṟṟi viṉippaṭukkuṅ kāl oruṅkeṉpatoru coṟpeytal ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉpa. piṟavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavuñ, ceyyuṭ kaṇiyāmeṉpatu avarkaruttu. oruṅkē yeṉpatēyaṉṟi mūṉṟu tāḻiciyuṇ mūṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi eṉavāṅku eṉpatoru collāṉ muṭintavaḻiyum eṉavāṅku eṉpatoru moḻi eṉaveṉpatu ōralaṅkāra meṉalvēṇṭumākalāṉ avvāṟu variyaṟuttuk kūṟalamiyāteṉpatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, avaṟṟip poruḷuṟuppeṉpatallatu aṇiyeṉpa vāyiṟ cāttaṉiyuñ cāttaṉālaṇiyappaṭṭa muṭiyuntoṭiyum mutalāyavaṟṟiyum vēṟu kaṇṭāṟpōla avvaṇiyuñ ceyyuḷiṉ vēṟākal vēṇṭumeṉpatu.
iṉic, ceyyuṭkaṇi ceyyum poruṭpaṭi ellāṅ kūṟātu cilavē kūṟi oḻiyiṉ atu kuṉṟakkūṟalā (663) meṉpatu. avi yāviyeṉiṉ.

kuṟuka vantutaṉ kūreyiṟu tōṉṟa	(akam.5)
eṉpatu, aṟivoṭu paṭātu pulampu koḷavanta ceyviṉi; eṉṉi? kēḷātu kēṭṭāṉpōl vantamiyiṉ. atuvum alaṅ kārameṉappaṭumeṉṟu cūttirañ ceytukoḷḷal vēṇṭuñ ceyyuṭku aṇi vēṟu kūṟiṉeṉpatu.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉavellāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷap paṭā; eṉṉi? vēṟu kāraṇamuṇarappeṟātu vuṟaḻntu iṭi yaṟavupaṭṭa kāli iṭarppaṭṭuc ceytaṉavātalāṉ aviyum avvāṟṟāṉamiyu meṉpatu.
iṉi āṉantavuvami yeṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟam akattiyaṉār ceytāreṉak kūṟupavākaliṉ aviyiṟṟi evvāṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, avikaḷ tām akattuḷḷum piṟacāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuḷḷum varutaliṟ kuṟṟamākā;akattiyaṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mūṉṟu tamiḻiṉum aṭaṅkāmi vēṟu āṉanta vōtteṉpatu oṉṟu ceytārāyiṉ akattiyamun tolkāppiyamum nūlāka vanta cāṉṟōr ceyyuṅ kuṟṟam vēṟupaṭāveṉpatu aḵtēṟ ceyyuṭku ivi yaṇiyeṉpatūum ivi kuṟṟameṉpatūum mūṉṟatikārattuḷḷum ivvāciriyaryāṇṭuṅ kūṟārōveṉiṉ, ceyyuḷiyalil muppattunāṉ kuṟuppuk kūṟi avaṟṟatiyipu nallicip pulavar ceyyuḷuṟup peṉavum avi toṭarnta toṭarnili eṇvakiyāṟṟoṭar nilic ceyyuṭku vaṉappeṉavum pakuttōtiya ilakkaṇattiṟpiṟaḻntavi ellāṅkuṟṟa meṉpatūum koḷḷavittāṉeṉpatu.
oruvari vilakka mūvarum vantilar eṉpuḻi oruvar eṉpavar ammūvaruḷ aṭaṅkumāṟupōṉṟu, cuṇṇamvari vilivitta mūṉṟu eṉpuḻic cuṇṇam eṉpatu, cuṇṇamum aṭimaṟiyum moḻi māṟṟum eṉac collappaṭṭa ammūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatām. cuṇṇam, aṭimaṟi, moḻimāṟṟu eṉpaṉa mūṉṟum cuṇṇampōṉṟu citarntu kiṭappaṉavātaliṉ, apporuḷkōḷ vakiyil uvamamum poruḷum vēṟuvēṟu citarntu kiṭappa uvamaṅkūṟiṉ avvuvamattāṟ poruḷviḷaṅkātu eṉpār, cuṇṇamvarinilivitta mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē eṉṟār.
tolkāppiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ vāḻnta kālattil tol kāppiya uvamayiyaluṭ kūṟappaṭṭa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiyum collatikārattilum ceyyuḷiyalilum kūṟappaṭum. cilaporuḷ kaḷiyum eṭuttukkoṇṭu avi ceyyuṭku aṇiyām eṉak koṇṭu tamiḻil aṇiyilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ pala tōṉṟalāyiṉa. vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷa taṇṭiyalaṅkārattiṉvaḻiyē tamiḻil taṇṭiyalaṅkāram eṉṟatōr aṇiyilakkaṇanūl tōṉṟiyatu. piṟkāla aṇiyilakkaṇanūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum aṇiyamitikaḷ pulamittiṟattil vallavarkaḷāṟceyyappaṭum ceyyuṭkaḷil aṇiyeṉa matikkap peṟṟum pulamittiṟattiṟ kuṟintavarkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭum ceyyuṭkaḷil aṇiyamiviṟ citiyā niliyilum aṇiyeṉa matikkappeṟāmalum varutaliṉ avvaṇiyamippukkaḷ yāvum uṟutiyākac ceyyuṭku aṇiceyvaṉa eṉaccollutaṟ killiyeṉpatu pērāciriyar karuttākum.
uvamiyeṉpatu poruḷpulappāṭṭiṟkuriya karuviyēyaṉṟi aḵtu aṇiyeṉappaṭātu. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiṟ kūṟappaṭum poruṭpakutikaḷ yāvum ceyyuṭku aḻakiṉittarum uṟuppukkaḷātaliṉ aviyellāvaṟṟiyumē tokuttu aṇi yeṉak kūṟutal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam ceyyuṭku aḻakuceyvaṉa aṉittiyum tokuttu aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu avaṟṟuḷ uvamam mutaliya cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum varinteṭuttuk koṇṭu avaṟṟi maṭṭum aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal payaṉil kūṟṟākum. aṇinūlkaḷuṭ kūṟappaṭuvaṉamaṭṭumaṉṟicceyyuṭku aḻaku ceyvaṉavēṟum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiyellām aṇiyeṉakkūṟutal vēṇṭum. ceyyuṭku iṉṟiyamiyāta aviyellāvaṟṟiyum cey yuḷiṉiṉṟum pirikka voṇṇāta poruḷuṟuppeṉpatallatu pirittuṇartaṟkuriya aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal poruntātu. cāttaṉum avaṉāl aṇintu koḷḷappaṭṭa muṭi toṭi mutalāyiṉavum vēṟātal pōlac ceyyuḷukkuriyavākac collappaṭum aṇikaḷum ceyyuḷiṉ vēṟātal vēṇṭum. ceyyuḷiṉiṉṟum pirikka voṇṇāta iyaṟkiyaḻakiṉip pirittuṇarakkūṭiya ceyaṟkiyiṉik kuṟikkum aṇiyeṉṟa peyarāṟ kūṟutal attuṇipporuttamuṭiyataṉṟu. iṉi, ceyyuṭku aḻaku ceyvaṉavāka iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa makavunili, kuṟippunili mutalākappulavaṉ mēlum vakutturikkattakka poruṭpaṭippukkaḷ ellām eñcāmal tokuttu avaṟṟi aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu avaṟṟuṭ cilavē kūṟiyoḻiyiṉ atu kuṉṟakkūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamāy muṭiyum eṉa ivvāṟu piṟkālattār kūṟiya aṇiyilakkaṇa amippiṉippērāciriyar maṟutturittār. eṉiṉum, ceyyuṭku aḻaku tarutaṟkuriya poruḷuṟuppukkaḷ iṉṉaveṉa uṇarappeṟāta nilimikkaṇ iṭarppaṭṭuc ceyyappeṟṟaṉa piṟkāla aṇiyilakkaṇa nūlkaḷātaliṉ aviyum oruvāṟṟāl amittuk koḷḷattakkaṉavēeṉap pērāciriyar avaṟṟukku amitiyum kūṟiyuḷḷami iṅkuk karutattakuvatākum.
āṉantavuvami yeṉpaṉa cilakuṟṟam akattiyaṉār kūṟiṉār eṉakkūṟupavar yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuriyāciriyarum āḷavanta piḷḷiyāciriyar mutaliyōrum āvar. avarkaḷāṟ kuṟṟameṉap paṭṭavi caṅkappāṭalkaḷuḷḷum piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷuḷḷum kāṇappaṭutalāl avi kuṟṟameṉappaṭā. aṉṟiyum akattiyaṉār tammāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa muttamiḻilakkaṇattuḷḷum aṭaṅkātavāṟu āṉantavōttu eṉapatoru nūl ceytārāyiṉ akattiyamun tolkāpiyamum nulāka vanta cāṉṟōr ceyyuṅ kuṟṟam an nūlkaḷiṉ vēṟupaṭutaṟku vaḻiyilli. eṉavē akattiyattiṟkum tolkāppiyattiṟkum māṟāṉ āṉantavōttu eṉṉum nūl akattiyaṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭataṉṟeṉpatum, annūlkaḷum āṉantavuvami mutalāyiṉa kuṟṟam eṉappaṭāveṉpatum naṉku pulaṉām.
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārceyyuḷiyaliṉ māttiri mutalāka muppattu nāṉkuṟuppukkūṟi avi nallicippulavar ceyyuḷuṟup peṉavum, avi toṭarnta toṭarnili ammi mutaliya eṇvakiyāl toṭarnilic ceyyuṭkuvaṉappāmeṉavum pakuttōti yaṉavellām ceyyuṭku aṇiyāmeṉpatum, avvilakkaṇaṅkaṭku māṟupaṭṭaṉa vellām kuṟṟamāmeṉpatum uṇarntu koḷḷavittār. ātaliṉ ceyyuṭku aṇiyāvaṉa iviyeṉavum kuṟṟamāvaṉa iviyeṉavum taṉitteṭuttuk kūṟavēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi tolkāppiyaṉārkku illiyeṉpatām.
āyvuri
itu, poruḷkōḷvakiyuḷ oṉṟākiya niraṉiṟipporuḷil uvamam varumāṟuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) uvamiyamittaṟku ovvāveṉavilakkum niliyil aminta cuṇṇam, aṭimaṟi, moḻimāṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷkōḷ mūṉṟumallāta nilikkaṇ, uvamayiyum niraṟpaṭa niṟuttip poruḷiyum niraṟpaṭa niṟutti uvamañceyyiṉ atu niral niṟi yuvamam eṉappaṭum. e-ṟu.
varinilivitta cuṇṇam (mutal) mūṉṟalaṅkaṭi niral niṟuttu amittal niral niṟi eṉa iyittupporuḷ koḷka. cuṇṇam---poṭi. citarntuḷḷa poṭipōṉṟu coṟkaḷ citarntu kiṭappa avaṟṟiyiyittup poruḷ koḷḷap paṭutaliṉ cuṇṇam eṉappeyar peṟṟatu. cuṇṇam eṉpatu, nāṟcīraṭi iraṇṭiṉkaṇ aminta eṇcīrkaḷip poruḷ iyipaṟintu kūṭṭiyuṇarum poruḷkōḷ. eṉavē, itaṉkaṇ uvamiyum poruḷum vēṟuvēṟu niṟka uvamañ ceytal poruḷ pulappāṭṭukku ovvātu. iṉi aṭimaṟiyeṉpatu, ceyyuḷiṉ ovvōraṭiyilum uḷḷa cīrkaḷ māṟātu niṟkac ceyyuḷiṉ ovvōraṭiyilum uḷḷa cīrkaḷ māṟātu niṟkac ceyyuḷiṉ aṭikaḷ maṭṭum mutalum iṭiyum maṟintu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutal. iṅṅaṉam aṭikaḷ muṉpiṉṉāka māṟum niliyil ōraṭiyil uvamaṅkūṟip piṟitōraṭiyil uvamēyattiṉi vittāl poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkātu. moḻimāṟṟu eṉpatu, ceyyuṭkaḷil muṉṉum piṉṉum aminta coṟkaḷip poruḷ iyiyumpaṭi iṭam māṟṟip poruḷ koḷḷutal. ōraṭiyuḷ uvamattiṉi vittup piṉpu ettuṇiyuṅkaṭantu maṟṟōraṭiyuḷ uvamēyattiṉik kūṟi moḻi māṟṟik koḷḷavippiṉ atuvum uvamattāṟ poruḷ pulappaṭātu. eṉavē collatikārattiṟkuṟitta nālvakip poruḷkōḷkaḷuḷ niraṉiṟiyoḻiya ēṉiya cuṇṇam, aṭimaṟi, moḻimāṟṟu eṉṉum mūṉṟum uvamaṅ kūṟutaṟku ovvātaṉa eṉa vilakkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpār, kaṇṇam vari nilivitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē yeṉṟār. āciriyar.
vari nilivittalāvatu uvamañceytaṟku ovvāta poruḷkōḷ iviyeṉa nīkkivittal.

4.	pulaṉ allātaṉa--itukāṟum poṟikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷkaḷ. pulaṉātal--viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutal. alaṅkāramātal--aṇiyuṭiyatātal.
1.	pākupaṭavarutalāvatu, kaṇmutaliya poṟikaṭkup pulaṉāvaṉavum, poṟikaṭkup pulaṉākātu maṉattiṟkup pulaṉāvaṉavum eṉa iruvakippaṭa varutal.
1.	oruporuḷōṭu oruporuḷi oppumi kūṟi viḷakkutal uvamam eṉappaṭum. akam puṟam eṉṉum iruvakipporuḷum uvamampaṟṟi vaḻakkiṉuḷ aṟiyappaṭutalāṉum muṟkuṟitta meyppāṭupōlapporuḷpulappāṭṭiṟku umamum ētuvākalāṉum uvamam paṟṟi 
iruvakipporuṭpakutiyum uṇarttuvatu uvamaviyalākum. akattiṇi iyaluḷ uvamattiṉi uḷḷuṟiyuvamam ēṉiyuvamam eṉa iruvakiyākkikkūṟiṉār. avviraṇṭiṉiyum virittukkūṟuvatu ivvuvamaviyalākum. ulakavaḻakkiluñceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum varuvatu ēṉiyuvamamākaliṉ ataṉi ivviyaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār. uḷḷuṟiyuvamam ceyyuṭkēyuriyatāyiṉum uvamap pakutiyātal oppumi nōkki ōriṉap poruḷākki itu ceyyuṭkurit teṉṉuṅ karuttāl aṭuttuḷḷa ceyyuḷiyalōṭum iyiyumpaṭi ivvuvamaviyaliṉ iṟutikkaṇ kūṟiṉār. eṉavē eḻuttikārattilum collatikārattilum ceyyuṭkuriya vitikaḷit taṉitteṭuttuk kūṟiyatupōlavē ipporuḷati. kārattum taṉitteṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷami oruvakiyāṟ pulaṉām.
yāppuṭiminōkki parappuṭimi nōkki eṉavum pāṭam
1.	nāṭaka vaḻakkuppōla ulakiyalvaḻakkum pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṟku uṟuppātaliṉ ulaka vaḻakkiṉi ārāytalum pāṭalcāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkuppayaṉ tarumātaliṉ ulaka vaḻakkuppaṟṟiya āyvum ivviyalil iṭam peṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
2.	āmā itu eṉakkaṇṭaṟiyātāṉukku āpōlum āmā eṉa uvamam kūṟiṉāl avaṉ kāṭṭuṭceṉṟuḻi āpōlum āmā eṉattāṉ kēḷviyuṟṟa avvuvamiyēpaṟṟi āmā itu eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟivāṉātalāl uvamattālum poruḷ pulappāṭē kūṟukiṉṟār. ākavē mēṟporuḷpulappāṭu kūṟiya meyppāṭṭiyalōṭu iyipuṭiyatu ivvuvamiyal ātaliṉ ataṉi aṭuttu vikkappeṟṟatu.
3.	(ivvōttiṉ talic cūttiram) - ivviyaliṉ mutaṟcūttiram.
1.	viṉipayaṉmey uru eṉṉumvippu muṟikkukakāraṇam kūṟuvatāka amintatu ivvurippakutiyākum. viṉiyāl viḷivatu payaṉātaliṉ itu viṉiyiṉ piṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. atupōl vaṭiviṉāl tōṉṟuvatu niṟamātaliṉ meyyiṉpiṉ uruvikkakap peṟṟatu. mey-vaṭivu. uru-niṟam. payaṉ eṉpatum poruḷātalāl meyyiṉiyum (vaṭiveṉṉum poruḷiṉiyum) ataṉuṭaṉ vittār. poruḷiṉ puṭipeyarcciyākiyaviṉiyuvamam ymayuvamattiṉ piṉṉarē kūṟattakkatāyiṉum viṉiyuvamam pulimaṟavaṉ eṉṟāṅku taṉṉurupu maṟintu nillātu puliyaṉṉamaṟavaṉ eṉa virintē niṟṟalum pulippāyttuḷ eṉa urupu maṟintu varutalum ākiya ciṟappuṭimi nōkki muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu eṉa viḷakkantaruvar pērāciriyar.
puliyaṉṉamaṟavaṉ eṉpatu,
pulipāyumāṟēpāyvaṉ eṉṉuntoḻil paṟṟi oppittamiyiṉ viṉiyuvamam.
1.	ākam - mārpu
	akaṉṟu kāṭṭutal-virintu tōṉṟutal
	aḵkittōṉṟutal-curuṅkit tōṉṟutal
2.	uvamattōṟṟam eṉpatu, uvamattāṟporuḷ tōṉṟum tōṟṟam eṉa viritaliṉ, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tokiyākum.
3.	vaṭivum  vaṇṇamum paṇpeṉa oṉṟāyaṭaṅkumāyiṉum mey (vaṭivu) paṟṟiya uṟṟuṇarum paṇpu iraviṉkaṇṇum uṟṟuṇarappaṭum kaṭpulaṉām paṇpākiya (25) vaṇṇam 
iraviṉkaṇ uṟṟuṇarappaṭātu. itu poruḷārāycci paṟṟi atikāramātalāl kaṭpulaṉām paṇpākiya vaṇṇattiṉiyum uṟṟuṇarum paṇpākiya vaṭiviṉiyum āciriyar vēṟākappakutturittār. ivvāṟu pakutturiyātu vaṭivum vaṇṇamum oṉṟākakkūṟiṉ pakaṟkuṟikkaṭkūṟappaṭum vaṇṇam mutalāyiṉavum paṇpeṉṉum potuvakiyāṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ eytuvāṉ cellum. aṅṅaṉam cellāmipporuṭṭum uvamattāl uṇarttakkarutiya tiṇipporuḷiṉi eḷitiṟpulappaṭuttal upakāram nōkkiyum mey (vaṭivu 25 (vaṇṇama) eṉa ivvāṟu pakutturittār tolkāppiyaṉār) aṭiciṉimutal (tol- col-26) eṉṟāṟpōla. viṉi, payaṉ, mey, uru eṉṉum innāṟpakutiyum paṇpeṉavē aṭaṅkakkūṟiṉ, paṇpu eṉpatu kuṟippiṉṟi nikaḻuṅ kuṇamākaliṉ pārvalotukkamākiya viṉi paṇpeṉappaṭātu māriyaṉṉavaṇki eṉavarum payaṉuvamiyippaṇpeṉa aṭakkiṉ paṇpāyiṉ māriyiṉ niṟampōṉṟu vaṇmiyum karitu eṉal vēṇṭum. ātalāl atu paṇpeṉa aṭaṅkātu. pāviyiyal kaṟṟaṉṉavoḻukkiṉaḷ eṉavaṭivupaṟṟi uvamaṅkoḷḷuṅkāl vaṭivupaṇpi (paṇpuṭiyaporuḷ) ātaliṉ, atu paṇ peṉappaṭātu. maḻikkālattiṟpūtta pīrkkampū cilavaṟṟiyeṭuttukkāṭṭi in niṟattaḷāyiṉaḷ talivi eṉṟa vaḻi, talivaṉ varuvatākakkuṟitta paruvaṅkaṭantu viṭṭatu eṉṉum poruṇmi viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iḵtu uruvuvamam, ivvāṟu viṉi payaṉ meyuru eṉṉum nāṉku pakutiyāṟ poruḷpulappaṭuttum muṟimi kaṇṭu uvamappakuti eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. aḷavu, cuvi, taṇmi, vemmi, naṉmi, tīmi, ciṟumi, perumi mutalāyiṉapaṟṟiyum uvamam varum. aviyellām iṅkukkūṟiya viṉipayaṉ mey uru eṉṉum innāṉkaṉuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavum avi nāṉkum ittakiya poruṭpakutiyiṉi yuṭiyaṉa eṉavum pulappaṭuttaṟ poruṭṭē avaṟṟi vakipeṟa vanta uvamattōṟṟam eṉa aṭi puṇarttōtiṉār āciriyar eṉpatām
1.	paṇpiḻipākaliṉ eṉpatu paṇpiyākaliṉ eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. kuṟitta paruvaṅkaḻintateṉṉum poruṇmi viḷaṅkiṟṟu uruvuvamam! eṉa mēluḷḷa toṭaruṭaṉ iyittu muṭikka.
2.	olittal viṉipaṟṟi eṉattiruttuka.
3.	nōkkiṉāṟ kolvāṉpōl kaṭikkaṇṇāl nōkkiṉaṉ, eṉpār kaṭikkaṇṇāṟ kolvāṉpōl nōkki eṉṟamiyiṉ itu viṉiyuvamattiṉ vakiyeṉappaṭum.

2.	poṉmaram pōlakkoṭukkum eṉpatu, ataṉāl koṭukkappaṭum koṭip poruḷuṭaṉ uvamaṅkoṇṭu meyyuvamamākātu, koṭuttal viṉiyāl koḷvārkkuvarum payaṉottalāl payaṉuvamattiṉ vakiyāyiṟṟu. maḻit toḻilākiya peyalāṟpayanta viḷiyuḷuṭaṉ iṭiyiṭṭup pōy uvamaṅ koḷḷātu eṉa ivvuriyiṭiyē kāṇappaṭuntoṭar, māriyaṉṉavaṇki eṉṟa toṭarkkuriya viḷakkamākat teritalāl, iṅkut toṭarpillāta ataṉi nīkkiviṭṭu, poṉmarampōlak koṭukkum eṉpatu payavuvamattiṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkum : eṉṉi? koṭipporuḷ iraṇṭum ottamiyiṉ 
meyyuvamam eṉappaṭātu koḷvārkkuppayam ottalāṟ payavuvamattiṉ vakiyāyiṟṟu. nilam pōlum koṭi eṉpatum atu eṉa iyittup paṭittal porutta muṭiyatākum. poṉmaram--kaṟpakam; intiraṉulakiṉ uḷḷa aintarukkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya kaṟpakam, taṉṉiyaṭintōr virumpikkēṭṭavaṟṟiyellām variyātu vaḻaṅkum teyvat taṉmiyuṭiyatu eṉpatu purāṇak koḷkiyākum.
1.	aḵtu aṉṉavākaliṉ eṉpatu aḵtu aḷavākaliṉ eṉṟiruttal poruṭporuttamuṭiyatākum. talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟuḷḷa iṭamum talivi taṅkiyirukkum maṉiyum ākiya iraṇṭiṉiyum ivaṟṟiṭiyēyuḷḷa elliyākiya iṭiveḷiyiyum uvami kūṟiṉamiyāl itu meyyuvamattiṟku vakiyeṉappaṭum eṉṟār pērāciriyar.
2.	tuṭiyiṭi eṉṟavaḻi mēlum kīḻum aminta iṭapparappum ataṉ iṭinuṇukkamum kuṇameṉappaṭātu vaṭivākavē koḷḷappaṭutal pōṉṟu, cēymi aṇmiyākiya nilapparappum kuṇameṉappaṭātu vaṭivākavē koḷḷappaṭum eṉpatām. iṉi aṉmiyum cēymiyum aḷavu eṉṉum kuṇamātal oppumiyāl avi niṟappaṇpiṟku iṉameṉavuṅkoḷḷappaṭum.
3.	iṉi aṇmiyum cēymiyum aḷavu eṉṉum kuṇamātal oppumiyāl avi niṟappaṇpiṟku iṉameṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. aṇmiyum cēymiyumākiya ivi ivvāṟu oruvakiyāl vaṭivākavākavum oru vakiyāl paṇpākavum tiripuṭimiyāl ivaṟṟit taṉitteṭuttukkūṟātu vaki eṉak koṇṭār āciriyar.
1.	taḷircivantāṅkuccivantamēṉi eṉpatu, niṟampaṟṟiya uvamiyāyilum civantāṅkuccivanta eṉa viṉivirittatupōl potuttaṉmiyiṉi virittamiyiṉ avvēṟupāṭu nōkki uruvuvamam eṉappaṭātu uruvuvamattiṉ vakiyeṉappaṭṭatu.

1.	avi-- muṉṉattāṉ uvamañceytaṉa.
1.	ceyyuḷ ceyvār uvamattāṟceyyum poruṭpaṭippakuti eṇṇiṟantaṉa vākaliṉ. appakutiyellām virittukkūṟiṉ perukumātaliṉ appakuti yellāṅkūṟātu potuvakiyāṉ variyaṟippaṭum ilakkaṇamē kūṟi, vāyeṉṟa pavaḷam, vāypavaḷam, vāypavaḷamāka eṉṟāṅkuvaruvaṉavaṟṟi muṉṉattiṉ uṇaruṅkiḷaviyākiya kuṟippuvamip pakutiyiṉkaṇ aṭakkikkūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār. ittakiyakuṟippuvamikaḷi ikkālattār uruvakameṉṟē vaḻaṅkuvar. iṅṅaṉam kuṟippiṉālvarum uvamikaḷuḷ tāmariyaṉṟumukamē eṉat tuṇintataṉi uṇmiyuvami yeṉavum, maḻiyaṉṟu vaṇṭiruttaliṟkuḻalē eṉapporuṭkuk kāraṇaṅkoṭuttalit tēṟṟavuvami eṉavum kūṟuvar taṇṭiyāciriyar. matiṅ kollō maṟuvilliyeṉṟu uvamikkukkuṟipāṭu kūṟutal vilakkuvamiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.

maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉaḵtuvameṉpa	(uvama - 9)
eṉappiṉvarum cūttirattut teḷivākak kūṟappaṭutalāṉum uvamattiṟkuriya nilikkaḷaṅkaḷ aintaṉuḷ oṉṟāka uvamēyattiṉik koḷḷutal poruntāmiyāṉum ivvurit toṭarum itaṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭappaṭum oṇceṅkaḻunīrkkaṇ pōlāyitaḻ (akanāṉūṟu-48) eṉavaruntoṭarum ic cūttirattiṟ kaminta iḷam pūraṇaruriyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷami ēṭeḻutuvōrāl nērnta piḻiyātal tiṇṇam.
1.	uvamikku nilikkaḷaṉkaḷāka muṉṉic cūttirattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa ciṟappu nalaṉ kātal vali eṉṉum nāṉkuṭaṉ iṅku aintāvatāka eṇṇappaṭuvatu, poruḷiṉ tāḻnta taṉmiyākiya iḻinili eṉṟalē poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu, mēṟcollappaṭṭa nāṉkum uyarviṉ pakutiyātaliṉ itaṉoṭuṅkūṭa ainteṉṟār eṉavarum iḷampūraṇam urit toṭarum, oru poruḷiṉ iḻipu kūṟuvāṉ uvamattāṉ iḻipu tōṉṟuvittaliṉ atuvum nilikkaḷamām eṉṟavāṟu eṉavarum pērāciriyar uriviḷakkamum iṅku oppunōkkiyuṇarattakkaṉavākum.
2.	oru poruḷiṉatu iḻiviṉikkūṟuvāṉ uvamiyiṉāl ataṉip pulappaṭut taliṉ avviḻiyum uvamikku nilikkaḷamāyiṟṟu.
1.	avvāṟu iṅku uvamikku niyamamilliyeṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu eṉṟiruttal poruṭporuttamuṭiyatākum. mutalukku mutalum ciṉikkuc ciṉiyum eṉac collatikārattiṟkūṟiya avvariyaṟi ceyyuḷiṟporuḷpulappāṭupaṟṟi varum ivvuvamikku vēṇṭuvataṉṟu eṉpār, nutaliyamarapiṉ uriyaviuriya eṉṟār.

1.	ilēcukoṇṭurippa eṉṟatu, collatikāram iḷampūraṇar uriyik kuṟittatu pōlum,

poruḷākkik kūṟutal. tāmaripōṉṟu malarntamukam eṉpuḻimukam eṉpatu uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ. tāmari eṉpatu mukattiṟku uvami. iṅṅaṉam uvamam kūṟutalē iyalpāṉamarapu. itaṉimāṟṟi mukamākiya poruḷi uvamam ākkiyum uvamiyākiya tāmariyi uvamikkappaṭum poruḷākkiyum tirumukam aviḻnta teyvattāmari eṉa uvamikūṟiya niliyil mukattiṉit tāmariyiṉum uyarvuṭiyatāka mayakkantīrac ciṟappitturittamiyāl kuṟṟamaṟṟa marapiṉ uvamiyāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam mukam okkuntāmari eṉapporuḷiyē uvamiyākkikkūṟuṅkāl ivviraṇṭaṟkum iṭiyēyaminta malarcciyākiya ciṟappuṭimi poruḷākiya mukattiṟkē mēlum ciṟappiṉittarutaliṉ uyarnta taṉmēṟṟu eṉa uvamikkukkūṟum avviti mukamākiya apporuṭkum eytu vikkappeṟum eṉpār, maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉ aḵtu uvamamākum eṉṟār. it toṭaril aḵtu eṉṟatu uvamikkappaṭum poruḷākiya mukattiṉiyātaliṉ uyarntataṉ mēṟṟē eṉa uvamikkukkūṟiya avvitiyi ataṟkum eytuvikka eṉṟār pērāciriyar.

1.	oppittal -- oppumi kūṟiviḷakkutal.

	iṅku eṇṇappaṭṭavuvamavurupukaḷ iṭiccollāy maṟintum virintum varutalum viṉic collāyp peyareccamākavum viṉiyeccamākavum muṟṟākavum vēṟupaṭa niṟṟalumākiya palavēṟu kuṟippukkaḷiyuṭiyaṉa eṉpār kūṟuṅkālip palkuṟippiṉa eṉṟār.
1.	iccūttirattil eṇṇappaṭṭa muppattāṟu urupukaḷum viṉipayaṉ mey uru eṉṉum nāṉku uvamattiṟkum potuvākum. iṉi nālvakiyuvamaṅkaṭkum eṭṭeṭṭākap pakutturikkappaṭum urupukaḷ avvav vuvamattiṟkuc ciṟappu vakiyāṉ uriyaṉa-iṅku eṇṇappaṭṭa muppattāṟaṉuḷ potumicuṭṭiya oṉṟa, eṉṟa, māṟṟa, poṟpa, nāṭa, naṭuṅka eṉpaṉa āṟurupukaḷum nīṅkalāka eñciyuḷḷa muppaturupukaḷōṭu piṟavum eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum urupukaḷuḷ nēra, nōkka eṉpataṉiyum kūṭṭa nālvakiyumaṅkaṭkum ciṟappuvakiyāṉ uriya urupukaḷ muppattiraṇṭāyiṉa.
puṟaṉaṭiyāṟ koḷvaṉa eṉṟatu ivviyal 10--ām cūttirattil eṭuttōtātu piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum nēra, nōkka, tuṇippa, maliya eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa vākiya uvamavurupukaḷi.
1.	uvamaccollē varampikantaṉavē eṉavarum innūṟpā, enta nūlukkuriya teṉak teriyavilli. tolkāppiyaṉār aṉṉamutal puriya eṉpatīṟākac colliya muppattāṟurupukaḷum piṟavum eṉpataṉāl accūttiravuriyuḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭa nēra eṉpatu mutal keḻuva eṉpatīṟākavuḷḷavurupukaḷum avi pōl arukivaruvaṉa cilavumāka uvamavurupukaḷi oruvarampiṟpakuttu avaṟṟi nālvakiyuvamaṅkaṭkup potumiyiṉ uriyaṉavākavum ovvoru vakikkuc ciṟappākavuriyaṉavākavum varumeṉavum, iṭic collākavum peyareccam viṉiyeccam viṉimuṟṟu eṉap palvēṟukuṟippiṉavākavum varum uvamavurupukaḷ yāvum, mēṟkuṟitta urupukaḷuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉavum koḷḷumāṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār uvamavurupukaḷi ivviyalil variyaṟuttukkūṟukiṉṟārātaliṉ, piṟarkūṟumāṟu pōla uvamaccol varam piṟantaṉa ākā eṉpatu pērāciriyar karuttākum.

2.	eḷḷa eṉpatu mutalākakkūṟappaṭṭa eṭṭurupukaḷum uvamiyum poruḷum payaṉvakiyāl ottaṉa eṉṟa kuṟippiṟpayaṉṟaṉavātaliṉ payaṉiliyuvamam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.

tōṉṟumaṉ eṉpuḻi maṉ eṉṉum iṭiccol ākkaṅkuṟittu niṉṟatu.
1.	aṉṉa eṉpatumutalāka eṭutturikkappaṭṭa uvamavurupukaḷi viṉip pāluvamam payaṉiliyuvamam, meyppāluvamam, uruviṉ uvamam eṉa nālvaki yuvamaṅkaṭkum evveṭṭākap pakutturittaṟkēṟṟa kāraṇam kūṟuvatu iccūttiramākum.
1.	nālvakiyuvamikaḷuḷ ivvuvamikku iṉṉavāypāṭukaḷ uriyaṉa eṉa mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu urimippaṭutturittaṟku avvuvamavurupukaḷ avvapporuḷ tōṉṟat toṉṟutoṭṭup payilappeṟṟuvarum varalāṟṟu muṟiyē kāraṇam eṉṟavāṟu.
2.	āṅku eṉṉum uvamavurupu viṉiyuvamattiṟkuriyatu. puli payntāṅku pāyntāṉ eṉpuḻi viṉiyuvamattiṟpayilutaṟkuriya ataṉ poruṇmi tōṉpṟutal kāṇalām, viṉiyuvamattiṟkuriya itaṉi taḷirāṅkuc civantamēṉi eṉa uruvumattiṟ cērnturittāl uvamapporuḷ viḷaṅkātu.
3.	eḷḷa eṉṉum urupu payaṉuvamattiṟkuriyatu. maḻiyeḷḷum vaṇmiyōṉ eṉavarum. iṅku maḻiyiṉi oruvaṉatu koṭikku iḻittuc colliṉum maḻiyiṉ payaṉum avaṉatukoṭip payaṉum ottaṉa eṉak koḷvataṉṟi am maḻiyiṉi avaṉukkut tāḻvuṭiyāka ulakattār iḻittu nōkkuvatilli. payiṉiliyuvamikkuriya eḷḷa eṉṉum ivvurupiṉi puliyeḷḷum pāyttuḷ eṉa viṉiyuvamattuḷ cērtturittal marapaṉaṟu. oruvaṉatu āṟṟali mikutturikkumiṭattu avaṉukku uvamiyākac colkiṉṟa puliyēṟṟiṉi avaṉik kaṇṭu etirniṟkalāṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭuttōṭiyatu eṉṟum naṭuṅkiṟṟu eṉṟum, avaṉippārkka añcik kaṇṇimittatu eṉṟum kūṟutal, ataṉāl uvamikkappaṭum vīraṉukkup pukaḻākum eṉpatē karutikkūṟiṉāl, kūṟiṉa avvumiyiṉ potuttaṉmi yākiya poruḷ veḷippaṭāmiyōṭu aṅṅaṉam añciyapuliyoṭu uvamittal avvīraṉukkum pukaḻākātātaliṉ payaṉuvamattiṟkuriya eḷḷa eṉṉum uvamarupiṉi viṉiyuvamattuṭaṉ cārttikkūṟutal uvamapporuḷ tōṉṟutaṟkuriya varalāṟṟu marapākātu eṉpatām.
1.	kaṭuppa eṉṉum urupu mevyuvamattiṟkuriyatākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. kaṭuttal eṉpatu aiyuṟutal. aiyam eṉpatu vaṭivu kaṇṭavaḻi piṟappatu. eṉavē ivvurupu meyyuvamattiṟ kuriyatu. viṉiyuvamampaṟṟip puliyoṭu maṟavaṉiyuvamikkuṅkālum oruvaṉatu koṭip payaṉi maḻiyiṉ viḷiviṉoṭu uvamikkuṅkālum vaṭivupaṟṟi aiyañceytal vēṇṭāmi yāṉum, poruḷiṉkuṇamākiya uruvu kaṇṭaniliyil aiyantōṉṟutaṟkiṭamiṉyilum, kaṭuppa eṉṉum urupu meyyuvamattiṟkēyuriyatāyiṟṟu. eyttal -- poruntutal, vaṭivoṭu poruntutal meyyuvamattiṟkuriyatākalālum maruḷa, puriya eṉpaṉa ituvō atuvō eṉa iraṇṭuṟa aiyuṟutalikkāṭṭutalāṉum oṭṭa oṭuṅka eṉpaṉa uvamamumporuḷum vaṭivāl oṉṟu eṉṟum poruṇmiyavākalāṉum nikarttal avviṉapporuḷ, eṉpataṉik kāṭṭutalāṉum ōṭa eṉpataṉ ōṭutaṟṟoḻil vaṭiviṟkaṉṟi niṟappiṇpiṟkiṉmiyālum ivvurupukaḷ meyyuvamattiṟku uriyavāyiṉa.
1.	uruvuvamavurupākiya pōlum eṉpatu, iṭiccollāy viṉic collākap payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkutalāṉ ataṟkuriya poruṭkāraṇam kūṟutaṟkiyalavilli. āyiṉum iḵtu aṉṉaveṉpatupōla maṟṟi mūṉṟuvamattum payiṉṟu varum eṉpatu eytuvittaṟku itaṉi uruvumattiṟkuriyavurupukaḷuḷ mutaṟkaṇ vittu eṇṇiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār . maṟuppa, oppa mutalāyiṉavaṟṟi uruvu mattiṟkup peruvaraviṉavāka āciriyar urimippaṭuttuk kūṟutalāl, aṅṅaṉam uriyaṉavātaṟku iviyum oru kāraṇamuṭiyatātal vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum. ivi marapiṟṟōṉṟum eṉṟataṉāl ivaṟṟi marapupaṟṟi aṟital vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu cūttirañceytalāṉ taliccaṅkattār mutalāyiṉār ceyyuṭkaḷuḷ avvāṟu payiṉṟu varumeṉpatu aṟintāmaṉṟē eṉa iyiyum. āciriyar ivvāṟu cūttirañ ceytamiyāl taliccaṅkattār mutalāyiṉār ceyyuṭkaḷuḷ ivvurupukaḷ ivvāṟu payiṉṟuvarum eṉpatu aṟintōmallavā? eṉpatu ittoṭariṉ poruḷākum.

2.	payavuvama vāypāṭukaḷ eṭṭaṉuḷ eḷḷa poruva, kaḷḷa, vella eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉ uvamam tāḻntatu eṉṉum poruḷiṉi yuṭiyavāy orukūṟāyaṭaṅkum, viḻiya, pulla. matippa, vīḻa eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamikkappaṭṭa poruḷiyuyarttutalum uvamiyit tāḻttalumiṉṟi aviyiraṇṭum tammuḷ ottaṉa veṉṟa poruḷiṉiyuṭiyavāy oru kūṟāyaṭaṅkum.

teḷimaruṅku--teḷintu tuṇiyum pakkam. poruṭṭiṟam palavākivarum ilakkaṇamāṉa mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu eṇvakimeyppāṭum paṟṟi uvamam koḷḷuṅkāl uvamāṉa aṭimoḻikku uvamēya aṭimoḻi kuṟintu varuvaṉavum yātum aṭimoḻi yaṉṟi varuvaṉavum ivviraṇṭum uvamēyattiṟku uṟṟa taṉmiyitu veṉavuṇaraviyalāmala amintaniliyilum avaṟṟiyuṇarntāṟkum uvamiyē teḷiyum pakkattatām eṉavum aṭimoḻiyiṉṟi uvami kūṟi uvamiyiṭi uvamēyattiṟkaminta potuttaṉmiyitu veṉa viḷaṅkavuṇarttāta niliyilum avvuvamiyē uvamēyattiṉ iyalpiṉit teḷintutuṇitaṟkuriya pakutiyatām eṉavum uṇarttuvār tiṟattiyalāṉa eṉṟār.

2.	aḵtu ētuvāka --avvaḻakkuk kāraṇamāka ataṉiyum--uvamēyap poruḷiyum
1.	eṉavē, ulakattu vaḻakkiṉuḷ aṭippaṭattōṉṟum uvamiyāyiṟṟāyiṉum puṟamatipōlumukam eṉṟatupōla uvamapporuḷ pulappāṭuceyyātu eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu eṉa ivvurittoṭari iyittup poruḷ kāṇka.

1.	uvami irāṇṭāki oru coṟṟaṉmippaṭṭum, avaṟṟāl uvamikkappaṭum. poruḷum irāṇṭāki orutoṭart taṉmippaṭṭumvariṉ ivi iraṭṭikkiḷavi eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu iraṭṭikkiḷaviyāyvarum uvamikkēṟpavē uvamēyamum iraṭṭik kiḷaviyāka amitalē poruḷpulappāṭṭaṟku ēṟpuṭiya ilakkaṇamarapām eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ karuttākum.
2.	kaṟṟār kallātār eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum uvamēyapporuḷkaḷ. makkaḷ vilaṅku eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum uvamikaḷ. kaṟṟār, kallātār eṉṉum uvamēyam iraṇṭiṉiyum kaṟṟārōṭu ēṉiyavar eṉa oru toṭarākavum, makkaḷ, vilaṅku eṉṉum uvami iraṇṭiṉiyum vilaṅkoṭu makkaḷ eṉa oru toṭarākavum koṇṭurippatu ittirukkuṟaḷātaliṉ iraṭṭikkiḷavi iraṭṭivaḻittē eṉpataṟku ilakkiyamāyiṟṟu. vilaṅkuc cātiyōṭum makkaḷōṭum uḷḷavēṟu pāṭuṭiyar ; viḷaṅkiyanūlkaḷik kaṟṟavarōṭu kallātavar. itu kallātār vilaṅku eṉṟatu eṉa ittirukkuṟaṭkup poruḷ varivar maṉakkuṭavar.
vilaṅkoṭu nōkka makkaḷ ettuṇi naṉmiyuṭiyar, attuṇit tīmiyuṭiya ṉarallar eṉpatām. mayakka niraṉiṟi eṉapporuḷ viḷakkantaruvar parimēlaḻakar. kaṟṟārōṭu ēṉiyavar, kaṟṟār, vilaṅkoṭu makkaḷ aṉiyar eṉṟō-amiyavēṇṭiya ittoṭarmuṟimāṟiyamintamiyiṉ mayakka niraṉiṟiyāyiṟṟu. mēl iruvakiyāka iyittukkāṭṭiya toṭarkaḷuḷ muṉṉataṉkaṇ kaṟṟārōṭu kallātārk kuḷḷa tāḻvum, piṉṉataṉkaṇ kallātārōṭu kaṟṟārkkuḷḷa uyarvum pulaṉātalaṟika.

1.	parutiyañcelvaṉ virikatirttāṉik
	kiruḷvaḷippuṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻil eṉavarum ittoṭar, maṇi mēkalik kāppiyattiluḷḷa paḷikkaṟipukka kātiyiṉ mutaliraṇṭaṭikaḷākum. ñāyiṟṟuc celvaṉākiya katiravaṉatu varinta oḷikkaṟṟiyākiya cēṉiyāl iruḷvaḷikkappaṭṭāṟ pōṉṟu aminta maruḷ eṉṉum icipāṭum vaṇṭukaḷ poruntiya malarkaḷiyuṭiya cōli eṉpatu ittoṭariṉ poruḷākum. itaṉkaṇ katiravaṉatu oḷi uḷḷē pukāvāṟu ceṟinta poḻilukku iruḷiṉ tiraṭciyiṉi uvamiyākak kūṟavanta āciriyar, katiravaṉatu oḷikkaṟṟiyākiya cēṇiyāl vaḷikkappaṭṭu oṉṟāyt tiraṇṭa iruḷiṉ tōṟṟat tiṉi uvamiyākak kūṟiyamil ituvēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟamāyiṟṟu. kūṟiyamaruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻikkoḷalāvatu, viṉipayaṉmeyuru eṉa muṉṉar uvamikku eṭuttōtiya neṟiyāṟ poruntum vaḻi amittukkoḷḷutalākum.

2.	uvami kūṟi avaṟṟi (avvuvamikaḷi) eṉappirittup poruḷuṇarka
1.	veṇkuṭikku uvamāṉamākiya matiyiṉi uvamēyap poruḷākki ataṉiyē yāṉikuttumeṉṟu vilakkiṉār.

2.	uvamiyum poruḷam avvaḻi māṟupaṭavarumāṟu eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
1.	itupōlum itu eṉa uvamivāypāṭṭāl oppumippaṭuttik kūṟutalēyaṉaṟi uvamiyatu taṉmiyiṉik kūṟutalum payaṉiliyuvamikkaṇ uvamiyātaṟkurittu eṉpatām.
	eṉavē iṅṅaṉam uvamiyatu taṉmi kūṟutal payaṉiliyuvamikkaṇṇēyē uvamiyākap pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟutaluritatu eṉpār, payaṉili yuvamikkaṇ eṉṟu koḷka eṉṟār.
1.	paṭṭāṅku uvamaṅkūṟutalāvatu, uvamiyum poruḷum uyarvutāḻviṉṟi uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyē iyalpiṉāl kūṟutal. uvamat taṉmiyum urittu. uvamam uyarntataṉ mēlatāy varutalaṉṟi uyarpiḻiyuṭiyatallāka taṉmiyuvamiyum urittu, payaṉili purinta vaḻakkattāṉa ataṉālum orupayaṉ tōṉṟac collutal neṟippāṭṭiṉkaṇ.
payaṉilipurinta vaḻakkattāṉa uvamattaṉmiyum urittu eṉamoḻipa eṉa iyiyum.
2.	ippāṭalil ulakiṉip pātukāttaṟku māriyi uvamittuc ciṟappittup pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉākiya pāriyi uyarttuk kūṟātārpōṉṟu iyalpāka uvami kūṟuvārāyiṉum, itu mārikkum pārikkum ulakupurakkuntiṟattil ōr uyarvutāḻvu illiyeṉṉum taṉmi tōṉṟak kūṟutaliṉ  itu payaṉili purinta vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. itu piṟar koḷkiyiṉi eṭutturittu maṟuttatu.
1.	taṭumāṟuvamam eṉappāṭaṅkoṇṭār pērāciriyar.
2.	taṭumāṟuvaral--atuvō ituvō eṉa aiyuṟṟut taṭumāṟum niliyil uvami varutal.
1.	itaṉ mutalaṭiyiṉit taṉic cūttiramākavum itaṉpiṉṉiraṇṭaṭiyiṉi vēṟōr cūttiramākavum koṇṭu urivarivar pērāciriyar.
2.	aṭukkiya tōṟṟamāvatu, veṇṭiṅkaḷ pōṉṟa caṅku pōṉṟa tāḻi eṉa ivvāṟu oṉṟaṟku oṉṟu uvamiyākat toṭarntu aṭukkivarutal. iṅṅaṉam potut taṉmi vēṟupaṭṭa niliyil uvamikaḷ aṭukki varuṅkāl avaṟṟiṭiyēyuḷḷa oppumi oru nikarttāy ottu vārāmiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa avvuvamikaḷāl uvamēyapporuḷiṉ iyalpu uḷḷavāṟu pulappaṭāmiyiṉ iṅṅaṉam aṭukkivarutal kuṟṟameṉa vilakkaṟpālatu eṉa aṟivuṟuttuvār, aṭukkiya tōṟṟam viṭuttal paṇpē eṉṟār āciriyar.
3.	niralniṟi cuṇṇam varaṉmuṟi vanta mūṉṟu eṉpaṉa muṉṉarc collatikārattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa niraṉiṟi mutaliya moḻipuṇariyalpunāṉkaṉuḷ uvami 
toṭartaṟ kiyalāta aṭimaṟi nīṅkalāka eñciya niral niṟi cuṇṇam, moḻimāṟṟu eṉṉum mūṉṟumākum. moḻipuṇariyalpākiya ivi allātaviṭattu aṭukkiyatōṟṟam viṭuttal paṇpu eṉavē, niralniṟi mutaliya ivi ākumiṭattup poruḷkōḷ vakiyil uvamikaḷ aṭukkivarutal kuṟṟamākātoṉpatām.
cuṇṇam vari nilivitta mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē eṉappāṭaṅkoṇṭu 			cuṇṇattiṉivarinta nilimiyāṉ vanta cuṇṇamum aṭimaṟiyum niralē niṟuttu amittal niralniṟi yuvamiyākum eṉapporuḷvarintār pērāciriyar.
1.	veṇṭiṅkaḷippōṉṟa caṅkiṉippōṉṟa tāḻi eṉa uvamikaḷi aṭukkikkūṟiṉāl veṇṭiṅkaḷi niṟattāl ottatu caṅku: caṅkiṉi vaṭivāl ottatu tāḻampū eṉavē veṇṭiṅkaḷukkum tāḻampūvukkum oppumiyiṉ miyiṉ aṭukki varalumiyāy vaḻuvāyiṟṟu.
